**[2792.42s] English:** There are weight division matches in which you compete against people roughly your own size and weight.

**[2805.18s] English:** There is an open weight division where anyone of any size can enter, so you can have very small people fighting very large people.

**[2813.56s] English:** And there is a second category.

**[2835.78s] English:** But in one category, you have one fight, the so-called "super fight," which is usually the headline fight of the event.
